Development Corporation General Regulations, 1975, 
namely 

1. (i) These regulations may be called the National Co¬ 

operative Development Corporation General (Amend¬ 
ment) Regulations. 1986. 

(ii) They shall be deemed to have come into force with 
effect from the date of issue of this notification. 

2. Eor regulation 29 of the National Cooperative Develop¬ 
ment Corporation General Regulations, 1975, the following 
shall be substituted, namely :— 

“29. Plaints, etc. by whom to be signed i —Plaints, written 
statements, petitions, vakalatnamas, affidavit and other 
documents connected with legal proceedings may be 
signed and verified on behalf of the Corporation by 
the General Manager, Deputy Director (Accounts) 
or Deputy Director (AdminiatrationJ, with the prior 
approval of the Managing Dffector. Besides the above 
officers, the Regional Directors are also authorised to 
sign and verify on behalf of the Corporation, the 
agreements, surety, mortgage and lease deed etc. for 
loans or advances to the employees of the Corpora* 
tion working in the Regional or Project Ottipes 
under their respective jurisdiction and for hirihg of 
office or residential accommodation by the Corppra* 
tion subject to the administrative approval of the 
competent authority’. 

R. V. GUPTA 
Managing Director 
NCDC 


OFFICE OF THE CENTRAL PROVIDENT FUND 
COMMISSIONER, 

New Delhi, the 1987 

No. P.IV/1(4)/S5.—In exercise of the powers conferred 
by sub-scction (7) of section 5p of the Employees’ Provident 
Funds and Miscellaneous Provisions Act, 1952 (19 of 1952), 
the Central Board, with the approval of the Central Govern¬ 
ment, hereby makes the following rules to amend the EmplO' 
yees’ Provident Fund Organisation Junior Librarian and 
Library Attendent Recruitment Regulations 1985 namely 

1. (1) These rules may be called the Employees’ Provident 

Fund Organisation Junior Librarian and Library 
Attendant Recruitment (Amendment) Regulations 
1987. 

(2) They shall come into force on the date of their pub¬ 
lication in the Official Gazette. 

2. In the Employees’ Provident Fund Chganisation Junior 
Librarian and Library Attendant Recruitment Regulations 
1985, for rule 6, the following shall be substituted namely i— 

"‘Saving Nothing in these rules shall affect reserva¬ 
tions, relaxation of age limit and other concessions 
required to be provided for the Scheduled Castes, 
the Scheduled TribeSj Ex-servicemen and other spe¬ 
cial categories of petaons. in accordance with the 
orders issued by the Central Government from 
time to time in this regard.” 

B. K. BHATTACHARYA 
Central Provident Fund Commissioner and 
Secy., Central Board of Tnistees,
